# Go API index

> Generated from the exported Go declarations. Do not edit this file by hand; run
> `cd tools/apiref && GOWORK=off go run . -root ../..` after changing the public API.

This is the exhaustive declaration index for Kayan's public Go API. Each entry links
to its canonical declaration and package documentation on pkg.go.dev. The linked
guide beside each package explains lifecycle, security behavior, errors, and usage.
Exported fields and interface methods are shown on the linked type declaration.

**Coverage:** 32 packages, 1961 exported symbols.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core`

Package core provides the foundation for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`type Credential`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core#Credential) - Credential represents stored authentication credentials.
- [`type ID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core#ID) - ID is the default identifier type used by Kayan (UUID).
- [`type Identity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core#Identity) - Identity is the default identity type.
- [`type Session`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core#Session) - Session represents an authenticated session.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent`

Package consent provides GDPR-compliant consent management for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`type Consent`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#Consent) - Consent represents a user's consent decision for a specific purpose.
- [`type ConsentError`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#ConsentError) - ConsentError is a consent failure with a machine-readable code.

- [`type ConsentHandlerFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#ConsentHandlerFunc)

- [`func ConsentsFromContext`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#ConsentsFromContext) - ConsentsFromContext retrieves consents from context.
- [`var ErrConsentExpired`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#ErrConsentExpired)
- [`var ErrConsentNotFound`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#ErrConsentNotFound)

- [`func HasConsent`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#HasConsent) - HasConsent checks if a purpose is consented in context.
- [`type Hooks`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#Hooks) - Hooks provides extension points for consent lifecycle events.

- [`method Manager.Check`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#Manager.Check) - Check returns whether consent is currently granted for a purpose.

- [`method Manager.ProcessExpired`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#Manager.ProcessExpired) - ProcessExpired handles expired consents.
- [`method Manager.Revoke`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#Manager.Revoke) - Revoke revokes a previously granted consent.
- [`method Manager.UpdateVersion`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#Manager.UpdateVersion) - UpdateVersion updates the policy version.
- [`type ManagerOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#ManagerOption) - ManagerOption configures the Manager.
- [`type MiddlewareConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#MiddlewareConfig) - MiddlewareConfig configures consent checking middleware.

- [`type PreloadConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#PreloadConfig) - PreloadConfig configures consent preloading middleware.
- [`func PreloadConsents`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#PreloadConsents) - PreloadConsents middleware loads consent data into context for downstream use.
- [`type Purpose`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#Purpose) - Purpose defines the type of consent being requested.

- [`const PurposeCommunications`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#PurposeCommunications) - Predefined consent purposes (GDPR/CCPA aligned).

- [`const PurposePersonalization`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/consent#PurposePersonalization) - Predefined consent purposes (GDPR/CCPA aligned).

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/device`

- [`method Manager.Evaluate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/device#Manager.Evaluate) - Evaluate assesses the trust level of a device for a given identity.
- [`method Manager.ListDevices`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/device#Manager.ListDevices) - ListDevices returns all devices registered to an identity.

- [`method Manager.RevokeAll`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/device#Manager.RevokeAll) - RevokeAll removes all devices for an identity.
- [`method Manager.Verify`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/device#Manager.Verify) - Verify marks a device as verified and upgrades its trust level to Medium.
- [`type ManagerOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/device#ManagerOption) - ManagerOption configures a Manager.
- [`type MemoryStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/device#MemoryStore) - MemoryStore is an in-memory implementation of Store for development and testing.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain`

Package domain defines core storage interfaces for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`type AuthToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#AuthToken) - AuthToken represents a temporary, expiring token used for flows like email verification, password recovery, or magic links.
- [`type BcryptHasher`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#BcryptHasher) - BcryptHasher hashes secrets with bcrypt.
- [`method BcryptHasher.Compare`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#BcryptHasher.Compare) - Compare reports whether password matches hash.
- [`method BcryptHasher.Hash`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#BcryptHasher.Hash) - Hash returns the bcrypt hash of password.
- [`type BulkSessionStorage`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#BulkSessionStorage) - BulkSessionStorage deletes every session belonging to an identity.
- [`type Clock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#Clock) - Clock reports the current time.
- [`type ClockFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#ClockFunc) - ClockFunc adapts an ordinary function to Clock.
- [`method ClockFunc.Now`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#ClockFunc.Now) - Now calls f.
- [`func ClockOrDefault`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#ClockOrDefault) - ClockOrDefault returns c, or SystemClock when c is nil.
- [`type CredentialStorage`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#CredentialStorage)
- [`const DefaultBcryptCost`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#DefaultBcryptCost) - DefaultBcryptCost is the work factor used when none is given.
- [`const DefaultTokenBytes`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#DefaultTokenBytes) - DefaultTokenBytes is the entropy, in bytes, of tokens from [NewTokenGenerator].
- [`var DefaultTokenGenerator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#DefaultTokenGenerator) - DefaultTokenGenerator produces [DefaultTokenBytes] of entropy from crypto/rand.
- [`var ErrConflict`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#ErrConflict) - Portable storage errors.
- [`var ErrExpired`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#ErrExpired) - Portable storage errors.
- [`var ErrNotFound`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#ErrNotFound) - Portable storage errors.
- [`type Hasher`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#Hasher) - Hasher defines the interface for password hashing and verification.
- [`type IDGenerator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#IDGenerator) - IDGenerator is a function that generates a new ID.
- [`type IdentityStorage`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#IdentityStorage) - IdentityStorage defines CRUD operations for identities and credentials.
- [`func NewBcryptHasher`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#NewBcryptHasher) - NewBcryptHasher returns a hasher with the given cost.
- [`func NewTokenGenerator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#NewTokenGenerator) - NewTokenGenerator returns a [TokenGenerator] drawing n bytes from crypto/rand and encoding them as base64url without padding.
- [`type SessionStorage`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#SessionStorage)
- [`type Storage`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#Storage) - Storage defines the interface for all persistence operations.
- [`type StrategyConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#StrategyConfig) - StrategyConfig represents the persisted configuration for a login strategy.
- [`type StrategyStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#StrategyStore) - StrategyStore defines the interface for persisting strategy configurations.
- [`var SystemClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#SystemClock) - SystemClock reads wall-clock time.
- [`type TokenGenerator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#TokenGenerator) - TokenGenerator produces a security token: an authorization code, refresh token, magic link, or state value.
- [`func TokenGeneratorOrDefault`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#TokenGeneratorOrDefault) - TokenGeneratorOrDefault returns g, or [DefaultTokenGenerator] when g is nil.
- [`type TokenStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/domain#TokenStore) - TokenStore defines the interface for managing transient authentication tokens.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow`

Package flow provides authentication flows and strategies for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`type APIKeyRepository`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#APIKeyRepository) - APIKeyRepository is the storage contract for the api_key strategy.
- [`type APIKeyStrategy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#APIKeyStrategy) - APIKeyStrategy implements machine-to-machine authentication via pre-shared API keys.
- [`method APIKeyStrategy.Authenticate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#APIKeyStrategy.Authenticate) - Authenticate verifies rawKey by hashing it and looking up the hash in storage.
- [`method APIKeyStrategy.ID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#APIKeyStrategy.ID)
- [`func AllowedAuthenticators`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AllowedAuthenticators) - AllowedAuthenticators accepts only the listed authenticator models, by AAGUID.
- [`func AsRateLimitError`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AsRateLimitError) - AsRateLimitError extracts RateLimitError from error if possible.
- [`type Attacher`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#Attacher) - Attacher is an optional interface for strategies that support linking to an existing identity.
- [`const AttestationAttCA`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AttestationAttCA) - Attestation formats an authenticator may report (WebAuthn Level 2, section 8).
- [`const AttestationBasic`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AttestationBasic) - AttestationBasic and AttestationAttCA are the forms that chain to a manufacturer root, which is the only kind that says anything about which device the credential lives on.
- [`type AttestationInfo`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AttestationInfo) - AttestationInfo describes a newly created credential, for a policy to judge.
- [`const AttestationNone`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AttestationNone) - AttestationNone means the authenticator vouched for nothing.
- [`type AttestationPolicy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AttestationPolicy) - AttestationPolicy decides whether a newly registered authenticator is acceptable.
- [`type AttestationPolicyFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AttestationPolicyFunc) - AttestationPolicyFunc adapts a function to [AttestationPolicy].
- [`method AttestationPolicyFunc.AllowAuthenticator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AttestationPolicyFunc.AllowAuthenticator) - AllowAuthenticator implements [AttestationPolicy].
- [`const AttestationSelf`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AttestationSelf) - AttestationSelf means the credential's own key signed the attestation.
- [`type AuditErrorHandler`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#AuditErrorHandler) - AuditErrorHandler receives persistence failures from an explicitly configured audit store.
- [`type BcryptHasher`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#BcryptHasher) - BcryptHasher hashes secrets with bcrypt.
- [`func CombineAttestationPolicies`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#CombineAttestationPolicies) - CombineAttestationPolicies requires every policy to accept.
- [`func CompositeKeyFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#CompositeKeyFunc) - CompositeKeyFunc chains multiple key functions.
- [`type ConfigurableRateLimiter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ConfigurableRateLimiter) - ConfigurableRateLimiter wraps any RateLimiter with additional callbacks.
- [`method ConfigurableRateLimiter.Allow`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ConfigurableRateLimiter.Allow)
- [`method ConfigurableRateLimiter.OnAllow`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ConfigurableRateLimiter.OnAllow)
- [`method ConfigurableRateLimiter.OnDeny`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ConfigurableRateLimiter.OnDeny)
- [`method ConfigurableRateLimiter.OnRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ConfigurableRateLimiter.OnRequest)
- [`method ConfigurableRateLimiter.Reset`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ConfigurableRateLimiter.Reset)
- [`type ContextKey`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ContextKey) - ContextKey is the type used for rate-limiter values carried in a context.
- [`func ContextKeyFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ContextKeyFunc)
- [`type CredentialSource`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#CredentialSource) - CredentialSource is an optional interface for models that support Kayan's discrete Credentials table.
- [`var DefaultPasswordPolicy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#DefaultPasswordPolicy) - DefaultPasswordPolicy enforces a minimum length of 8 and maximum of 128 with no complexity requirements.
- [`var ErrAPIKeyExpired`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrAPIKeyExpired)
- [`var ErrAPIKeyInvalid`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrAPIKeyInvalid) - API Key errors.
- [`var ErrAPIKeyScopeInsufficient`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrAPIKeyScopeInsufficient)
- [`var ErrAccountInactive`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrAccountInactive) - ErrAccountInactive is returned after credentials verify when the identity is disabled, locked, or pending.
- [`var ErrAttestationMissing`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrAttestationMissing) - ErrAttestationMissing reports a registration that carried no usable attestation where the policy required one.
- [`var ErrAuthenticatorNotAllowed`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrAuthenticatorNotAllowed) - ErrAuthenticatorNotAllowed reports an authenticator model the policy does not accept.
- [`var ErrIdentityAlreadyExists`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrIdentityAlreadyExists) - ErrIdentityAlreadyExists is returned when a registration attempt matches an existing identity but the duplicate policy prevents automatic linking/capture.
- [`var ErrKayanOIDCMissingIDToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrKayanOIDCMissingIDToken)
- [`var ErrKayanOIDCNonceMismatch`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrKayanOIDCNonceMismatch)
- [`var ErrKayanOIDCStateExpired`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrKayanOIDCStateExpired)
- [`var ErrKayanOIDCStateInvalid`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrKayanOIDCStateInvalid) - Kayan OIDC errors.
- [`var ErrKayanOIDCTokenInvalid`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrKayanOIDCTokenInvalid)
- [`var ErrLDAPAmbiguousUser`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrLDAPAmbiguousUser) - ErrLDAPAmbiguousUser reports that the username matched more than one directory entry.
- [`var ErrLDAPConnectionFailed`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrLDAPConnectionFailed)
- [`var ErrLDAPInvalidCredentials`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrLDAPInvalidCredentials) - LDAP errors.
- [`var ErrLDAPResultTruncated`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrLDAPResultTruncated) - ErrLDAPResultTruncated reports that the directory returned fewer entries than matched the filter, because a size limit stopped it.
- [`var ErrLDAPSearchFailed`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrLDAPSearchFailed) - ErrLDAPSearchFailed reports that the directory refused or could not complete the user search.
- [`var ErrLDAPUserNotFound`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrLDAPUserNotFound)
- [`var ErrMFARequired`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrMFARequired)
- [`var ErrNoDiscoverableUserLoader`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrNoDiscoverableUserLoader) - ErrNoDiscoverableUserLoader reports that a discoverable login was attempted with no way to resolve the user handle.
- [`var ErrNoRecoveryCodesRemaining`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrNoRecoveryCodesRemaining)
- [`var ErrPasswordNoDigit`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrPasswordNoDigit) - Password policy sentinel errors.
- [`var ErrPasswordNoLower`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrPasswordNoLower) - Password policy sentinel errors.
- [`var ErrPasswordNoSpecial`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrPasswordNoSpecial) - Password policy sentinel errors.
- [`var ErrPasswordNoUpper`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrPasswordNoUpper) - Password policy sentinel errors.
- [`var ErrPasswordTooLong`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrPasswordTooLong) - Password policy sentinel errors.
- [`var ErrPasswordTooShort`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrPasswordTooShort) - Password policy sentinel errors.
- [`var ErrRecoveryCodeAlreadyUsed`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrRecoveryCodeAlreadyUsed)
- [`var ErrRecoveryCodeInvalid`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrRecoveryCodeInvalid) - Recovery code errors.
- [`var ErrRecoveryRateLimited`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrRecoveryRateLimited) - ErrRecoveryRateLimited is returned when recovery requests exceed the rate limit.
- [`var ErrStepUpNoPolicy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrStepUpNoPolicy) - Step-up errors.
- [`var ErrTOTPCodeInvalid`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrTOTPCodeInvalid) - ErrTOTPCodeInvalid is returned when the TOTP code does not match within any valid time window.
- [`var ErrTOTPReplay`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrTOTPReplay) - ErrTOTPReplay is returned when the TOTP time-step counter was already used (replay attack).
- [`var ErrTOTPSecretNotFound`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrTOTPSecretNotFound) - ErrTOTPSecretNotFound is returned when no TOTP secret is configured for the identity.
- [`var ErrUnknownDiscoverableCredential`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrUnknownDiscoverableCredential) - ErrUnknownDiscoverableCredential reports a user handle that resolved to no identity.
- [`var ErrUserHandleMismatch`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrUserHandleMismatch) - ErrUserHandleMismatch reports an identity whose ID is not the user handle the authenticator asserted.
- [`var ErrWebAuthnClonedCredential`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#ErrWebAuthnClonedCredential) - WebAuthn errors.
- [`type FixedWindowRateLimiter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#FixedWindowRateLimiter) - FixedWindowRateLimiter implements rate limiting using fixed time windows.

- [`method FixedWindowRateLimiter.Reset`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#FixedWindowRateLimiter.Reset)
- [`type Flow`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#Flow) - Flow represents a transient state for an authentication process.
- [`type FlowIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#FlowIdentity) - FlowIdentity defines the minimum interface that any identity model must satisfy.
- [`type FlowType`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#FlowType) - FlowType represents the type of authentication flow.

- [`func GenerateAPIKey`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#GenerateAPIKey) - GenerateAPIKey generates a cryptographically random API key of the given byte length and returns both the raw key (to show to the user once) and its hash (to store).
- [`func GenerateRecoveryCodes`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#GenerateRecoveryCodes) - GenerateRecoveryCodes generates n cryptographically random hex codes and their bcrypt hashes.
- [`func HashAPIKey`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#HashAPIKey) - HashAPIKey returns the hex-encoded SHA-256 hash of rawKey.
- [`type Hook`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#Hook) - Hook defines a function that runs before or after a flow action.
- [`type IDTokenClaims`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#IDTokenClaims) - IDTokenClaims carries the verified claims from a Kayan-issued ID token.
- [`type IDTokenParser`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#IDTokenParser) - IDTokenParser verifies a raw OIDC ID token JWT and returns its claims.
- [`func IPKeyFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#IPKeyFunc) - IPKeyFunc creates a key function that extracts IP from identifier.
- [`type IdentityRepository`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#IdentityRepository) - IdentityRepository is an alias for domain.IdentityStorage.
- [`type IdentityStateSource`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#IdentityStateSource) - IdentityStateSource lets an identity opt into central account-state enforcement.
- [`type Initiator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#Initiator) - Initiator is an optional interface for login strategies that support a multi-step initiation (e.g.
- [`func IsRateLimitError`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#IsRateLimitError) - IsRateLimitError checks if an error is a rate limit error.
- [`type KayanOIDCRepository`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#KayanOIDCRepository) - KayanOIDCRepository is the storage contract for the kayan_oidc strategy.
- [`type KayanOIDCStrategy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#KayanOIDCStrategy) - KayanOIDCStrategy authenticates users via a Kayan instance acting as the OIDC provider.
- [`method KayanOIDCStrategy.Authenticate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#KayanOIDCStrategy.Authenticate) - Authenticate handles the OIDC callback: exchanges the code, verifies the ID token, and resolves (or creates) the local Kayan identity.
- [`method KayanOIDCStrategy.ID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#KayanOIDCStrategy.ID)
- [`method KayanOIDCStrategy.Initiate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#KayanOIDCStrategy.Initiate) - Initiate generates state + PKCE verifier + nonce, stores them, and returns the authorization URL to redirect the user to.
- [`type LDAPConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPConfig) - LDAPConfig holds the configuration for the ldap strategy.
- [`type LDAPConn`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPConn) - LDAPConn is a minimal interface over an active LDAP connection.
- [`type LDAPDialer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPDialer) - LDAPDialer is the interface for opening an LDAP connection.

- [`type LDAPExternalBinder`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPExternalBinder) - LDAPExternalBinder is implemented by connections that can bind with SASL EXTERNAL.

- [`const LDAPServiceAuthExternal`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPServiceAuthExternal) - LDAPServiceAuthExternal binds the service account with SASL EXTERNAL, taking its identity from the TLS client certificate (RFC 4422 appendix A).
- [`const LDAPServiceAuthSimple`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPServiceAuthSimple) - LDAPServiceAuthSimple binds the service account with a DN and password.
- [`type LDAPStrategy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPStrategy) - LDAPStrategy is a single-step LoginStrategy that authenticates against an LDAP / Active Directory server.
- [`method LDAPStrategy.Authenticate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPStrategy.Authenticate) - Authenticate looks up the user in LDAP (via service-account bind) then re-binds as that user to verify the supplied password.
- [`method LDAPStrategy.ID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LDAPStrategy.ID)
- [`type Linker`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#Linker) - Linker handles account linking and identity unification.
- [`type LockoutConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LockoutConfig) - LockoutConfig holds configuration for the lockout decorator.

- [`type LockoutStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/flow#LockoutStore) - LockoutStore defines the storage for tracking login failures and lockouts.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys`

Package keys defines how Kayan obtains signing keys and produces signed tokens.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`func BuildJWKS`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#BuildJWKS) - BuildJWKS assembles the key set to publish at the JWKS endpoint.
- [`var ErrInvalidKey`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#ErrInvalidKey) - ErrInvalidKey reports a key that is missing a required field.
- [`var ErrKeyNotFound`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#ErrKeyNotFound) - ErrKeyNotFound reports that no key matches the requested key ID.
- [`var ErrMalformedJWK`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#ErrMalformedJWK) - ErrMalformedJWK reports a key that could not be read as one.
- [`var ErrNoKey`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#ErrNoKey) - ErrNoKey reports that no key is available for signing.
- [`var ErrSymmetricJWK`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#ErrSymmetricJWK) - ErrSymmetricJWK reports an "oct" key where a public key was required.
- [`var ErrUnsupportedKey`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#ErrUnsupportedKey) - ErrUnsupportedKey reports a key type this package cannot represent as a JWK.
- [`type JWK`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#JWK) - JWK is a JSON Web Key (RFC 7517).
- [`method JWK.PublicKey`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#JWK.PublicKey) - PublicKey converts j into a usable public key.
- [`type JWKS`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#JWKS) - JWKS is a JSON Web Key Set (RFC 7517 section 5).
- [`method JWKS.Find`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#JWKS.Find) - Find returns the key with the given kid.
- [`type JWTSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#JWTSigner) - JWTSigner signs claims with the active key from a [Provider].
- [`method JWTSigner.Sign`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#JWTSigner.Sign) - Sign implements [Signer].
- [`type Key`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#Key) - Key is a single signing key together with the algorithm it is used with.
- [`method Key.Algorithm`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#Key.Algorithm) - Algorithm returns the JWS algorithm name, or "" when the key has no method.
- [`method Key.CanSign`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#Key.CanSign) - CanSign reports whether k carries private key material.
- [`method Key.Validate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#Key.Validate) - Validate reports whether k is usable.
- [`func KeyToJWK`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#KeyToJWK) - KeyToJWK converts the public half of k to a JWK.
- [`func Keyfunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#Keyfunc) - Keyfunc returns a [jwt.Keyfunc] that resolves the verification key by the token's "kid" header.
- [`func NewJWTSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#NewJWTSigner) - NewJWTSigner returns a Signer that signs with p's active key.
- [`func NewStaticProvider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#NewStaticProvider) - NewStaticProvider returns a Provider serving active for signing, and active plus retired for verification.
- [`func NewStaticProviderWithError`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#NewStaticProviderWithError) - NewStaticProviderWithError is [NewStaticProvider] that reports an error instead of panicking.
- [`func ParseJWKS`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#ParseJWKS) - ParseJWKS reads a JSON Web Key Set (RFC 7517 section 5).
- [`type Provider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#Provider) - Provider supplies signing keys.
- [`func PublicKeyOf`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#PublicKeyOf) - PublicKeyOf derives the public key for k, falling back to deriving it from the private key when Public is unset.
- [`type Signer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#Signer) - Signer produces a signed compact JWS.
- [`type SignerOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#SignerOption) - SignerOption configures a [JWTSigner].
- [`type StaticProvider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#StaticProvider) - StaticProvider serves a fixed set of keys held in memory.
- [`method StaticProvider.Active`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#StaticProvider.Active) - Active implements [Provider].
- [`method StaticProvider.ByKID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#StaticProvider.ByKID) - ByKID implements [Provider].
- [`method StaticProvider.Rotate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#StaticProvider.Rotate) - Rotate promotes next to the active key, keeping the previous active key for verification.
- [`method StaticProvider.Verification`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#StaticProvider.Verification) - Verification implements [Provider].
- [`const UseEncryption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#UseEncryption) - Use values for [Key.Use], per RFC 7517 section 4.2.
- [`const UseSignature`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/keys#UseSignature) - Use values for [Key.Use], per RFC 7517 section 4.2.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa`

Package mfa provides unified Multi-Factor Authentication orchestration for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`type Challenge`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Challenge) - Challenge represents a pending MFA verification request.
- [`type Enrollment`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Enrollment) - Enrollment represents a configured MFA method for an identity.
- [`const EnrollmentActive`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#EnrollmentActive) - EnrollmentActive indicates enrollment is confirmed and active.
- [`const EnrollmentDisabled`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#EnrollmentDisabled) - EnrollmentDisabled indicates enrollment has been disabled.
- [`const EnrollmentPending`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#EnrollmentPending) - EnrollmentPending indicates enrollment has been initiated but not confirmed.
- [`type EnrollmentStatus`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#EnrollmentStatus) - EnrollmentStatus represents the state of an MFA enrollment.
- [`type MFAStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MFAStore) - MFAStore defines the interface for persisting MFA enrollments, challenges, and recovery codes.
- [`type Manager`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager) - Manager provides a unified API for MFA enrollment, challenge, and verification.
- [`method Manager.Challenge`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.Challenge) - Challenge creates an MFA challenge for an identity using the first active enrollment.
- [`method Manager.ChallengeWithMethod`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.ChallengeWithMethod) - ChallengeWithMethod creates an MFA challenge for a specific method.
- [`method Manager.ConfirmEnrollment`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.ConfirmEnrollment) - ConfirmEnrollment verifies and activates a pending enrollment.
- [`method Manager.DisableMethod`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.DisableMethod) - DisableMethod disables an MFA enrollment.
- [`method Manager.Enroll`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.Enroll) - Enroll initiates enrollment for an identity in a specific MFA method.
- [`method Manager.GenerateRecoveryCodes`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.GenerateRecoveryCodes) - GenerateRecoveryCodes generates one-time-use backup codes for an identity.
- [`method Manager.ListEnrollments`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.ListEnrollments) - ListEnrollments returns all MFA enrollments for an identity.
- [`method Manager.RegisterMethod`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.RegisterMethod) - RegisterMethod registers an MFA method with the manager.
- [`method Manager.Verify`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.Verify) - Verify checks the user's response against a pending challenge.
- [`method Manager.VerifyRecoveryCode`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Manager.VerifyRecoveryCode) - VerifyRecoveryCode checks and consumes a recovery code.
- [`type ManagerOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#ManagerOption) - ManagerOption configures a Manager.
- [`type MemoryStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore) - MemoryStore is an in-memory implementation of MFAStore for development and testing.
- [`method MemoryStore.ConsumeRecoveryCode`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore.ConsumeRecoveryCode)

- [`method MemoryStore.DeleteEnrollment`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore.DeleteEnrollment)
- [`method MemoryStore.GetChallenge`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore.GetChallenge)
- [`method MemoryStore.GetEnrollment`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore.GetEnrollment)
- [`method MemoryStore.GetEnrollmentsByIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore.GetEnrollmentsByIdentity)
- [`method MemoryStore.GetRecoveryCodes`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore.GetRecoveryCodes)

- [`method MemoryStore.SaveRecoveryCodes`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore.SaveRecoveryCodes)
- [`method MemoryStore.UpdateEnrollment`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#MemoryStore.UpdateEnrollment)
- [`type Method`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/mfa#Method) - Method represents an MFA method that can be enrolled and challenged.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy`

Package policy provides authorization engines for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`type ABACStrategy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#ABACStrategy) - ABACStrategy implements Attribute-Based Access Control.
- [`method ABACStrategy.AddRule`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#ABACStrategy.AddRule) - AddRule registers a new policy rule for a specific action.
- [`method ABACStrategy.Can`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#ABACStrategy.Can)
- [`const AllowOverrides`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#AllowOverrides) - AllowOverrides: If any engine allows (returns true), result is true.
- [`type AuditMiddleware`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#AuditMiddleware)
- [`method AuditMiddleware.Can`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#AuditMiddleware.Can)
- [`method AuditMiddleware.Wait`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#AuditMiddleware.Wait) - Wait blocks until in-flight asynchronous audit writes have finished.
- [`type AuditOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#AuditOption) - AuditOption configures an [AuditMiddleware].
- [`type CacheKeyer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#CacheKeyer) - CacheKeyer lets a subject or resource state its own cache identity.
- [`type CachingMiddleware`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#CachingMiddleware) - CachingMiddleware caches authorization decisions for a bounded time.
- [`method CachingMiddleware.Can`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#CachingMiddleware.Can)
- [`method CachingMiddleware.Invalidate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#CachingMiddleware.Invalidate) - Invalidate clears the cache.
- [`type CachingOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#CachingOption) - CachingOption configures a [CachingMiddleware].
- [`type Combinator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#Combinator) - Combinator defines how multiple policies are combined.
- [`type Context`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#Context) - Context is a flexible map for passing environmental or additional data to the policy engine.
- [`const DefaultAuditConcurrency`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#DefaultAuditConcurrency) - DefaultAuditConcurrency bounds concurrent asynchronous audit writes.
- [`const DefaultMaxCacheEntries`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#DefaultMaxCacheEntries) - DefaultMaxCacheEntries bounds the decision cache when no limit is given.
- [`const DenyOverrides`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#DenyOverrides) - DenyOverrides: If any engine denies (returns false), result is false.
- [`type Engine`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#Engine) - Engine defines the core interface for making authorization decisions.
- [`type Factory`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#Factory) - Factory defines a function that creates a new policy engine instance.
- [`type HybridStrategy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#HybridStrategy) - HybridStrategy allows combining multiple authorization strategies.
- [`method HybridStrategy.Can`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#HybridStrategy.Can)
- [`func NewABACStrategy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#NewABACStrategy)
- [`func NewAuditMiddleware`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#NewAuditMiddleware)
- [`func NewCachingMiddleware`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#NewCachingMiddleware)
- [`func NewHybridStrategy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#NewHybridStrategy) - NewHybridStrategy creates a composed strategy.
- [`var PolicyContextKey`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#PolicyContextKey)
- [`type Rule`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#Rule) - Rule defines a function that evaluates a policy decision.
- [`func WithAsyncAudit`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#WithAsyncAudit) - WithAsyncAudit records decisions on a background goroutine so the authorization check does not wait for the store.
- [`func WithAuditConcurrency`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#WithAuditConcurrency) - WithAuditConcurrency bounds concurrent asynchronous writes.
- [`func WithAuditDropWhenSaturated`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#WithAuditDropWhenSaturated) - WithAuditDropWhenSaturated drops audit records instead of blocking when the concurrency bound is reached.
- [`func WithAuditErrorHandler`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#WithAuditErrorHandler) - WithAuditErrorHandler reports audit write failures.
- [`func WithCacheClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#WithCacheClock) - WithCacheClock sets the clock used for expiry.
- [`func WithContext`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#WithContext)
- [`func WithMaxCacheEntries`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/policy#WithMaxCacheEntries) - WithMaxCacheEntries bounds the number of cached decisions.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac`

Package rbac provides Role-Based Access Control for Kayan IAM.

- [`func AnyPermissionGranted`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#AnyPermissionGranted) - AnyPermissionGranted reports whether any of the granted permissions covers the requested one.

- [`method BasicStrategy.GetRoles`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#BasicStrategy.GetRoles)

- [`var ErrCycle`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#ErrCycle) - ErrCycle reports a cycle in role inheritance.
- [`var ErrRoleNotFound`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#ErrRoleNotFound) - ErrRoleNotFound reports a role that has no definition.
- [`type IdentityLoader`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#IdentityLoader) - IdentityLoader loads an identity or subject object by ID.
- [`type Manager`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#Manager) - Manager handles authorization checks using a configured strategy.
- [`method Manager.Authorize`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#Manager.Authorize) - Authorize checks if the given identity has the required role.
- [`method Manager.AuthorizePermission`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#Manager.AuthorizePermission) - AuthorizePermission checks if the given identity has the required permission.
- [`method Manager.GetPermissions`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#Manager.GetPermissions) - GetPermissions returns all permissions for the given identity.
- [`method Manager.GetRoles`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#Manager.GetRoles) - GetRoles returns all roles for the given identity.
- [`method Manager.RequirePermission`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#Manager.RequirePermission) - RequirePermission is a helper that returns an error if the identity does not have the permission.
- [`method Manager.RequireRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#Manager.RequireRole) - RequireRole is a helper that returns an error if the identity does not have the role.
- [`const MaxInheritanceDepth`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MaxInheritanceDepth) - MaxInheritanceDepth bounds how far role inheritance is followed.

- [`method MemoryStrategy.AddRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.AddRole) - AddRole registers a role definition without validating inheritance.
- [`method MemoryStrategy.AssignRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.AssignRole) - AssignRole grants a role to an identity.
- [`method MemoryStrategy.DefineRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.DefineRole) - DefineRole registers a role definition.
- [`method MemoryStrategy.DeleteRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.DeleteRole) - DeleteRole implements [RoleStore].
- [`method MemoryStrategy.GetPermissions`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.GetPermissions) - GetPermissions implements [Strategy], including inherited permissions.
- [`method MemoryStrategy.GetRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.GetRole) - GetRole implements [RoleStore].
- [`method MemoryStrategy.GetRoles`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.GetRoles) - GetRoles implements [Strategy].
- [`method MemoryStrategy.HasPermission`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.HasPermission) - HasPermission implements [Strategy].
- [`method MemoryStrategy.HasRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.HasRole) - HasRole implements [Strategy].
- [`method MemoryStrategy.ListRoles`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.ListRoles) - ListRoles implements [RoleStore].
- [`method MemoryStrategy.RevokeRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.RevokeRole) - RevokeRole removes a role from an identity.
- [`method MemoryStrategy.SaveRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.SaveRole) - SaveRole implements [RoleStore].
- [`method MemoryStrategy.UnassignRole`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#MemoryStrategy.UnassignRole) - UnassignRole removes a role from an identity.

- [`func NewManager`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#NewManager) - NewManager creates a new RBAC Manager with the given strategy.

- [`func PermissionGranted`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#PermissionGranted) - PermissionGranted reports whether a granted permission covers a requested one.
- [`const PermissionSeparator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rbac#PermissionSeparator) - PermissionSeparator divides the segments of a permission string.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac`

Package rebac implements Relationship-Based Access Control (ReBAC).

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`type Checker`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Checker) - Checker performs permission checks by traversing the relation graph.
- [`method Checker.Check`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Checker.Check) - Check returns true if the subject has the specified relation to the object.
- [`type CheckerOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#CheckerOption) - CheckerOption configures a Checker.
- [`type ComputedRule`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#ComputedRule) - ComputedRule defines a way to compute a relation from existing data.
- [`const DefaultMaxDepth`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#DefaultMaxDepth) - DefaultMaxDepth is the default maximum recursion depth for permission checks.
- [`func DefaultObjectExtractor`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#DefaultObjectExtractor) - DefaultObjectExtractor handles common resource types.
- [`func DefaultSubjectExtractor`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#DefaultSubjectExtractor) - DefaultSubjectExtractor handles common subject types.
- [`type Manager`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager) - Manager provides a high-level API for ReBAC operations.
- [`method Manager.AddToGroup`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.AddToGroup) - AddToGroup adds a user as a member of a group.
- [`method Manager.Can`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.Can) - Can implements policy.Engine interface.
- [`method Manager.Check`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.Check) - Check verifies if a subject has a specific relation to an object.
- [`method Manager.GetParent`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.GetParent) - GetParent returns the parent of a resource, if any.
- [`method Manager.Grant`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.Grant) - Grant creates a relationship tuple.
- [`method Manager.GrantUserset`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.GrantUserset) - GrantUserset creates a relationship tuple with a userset subject.
- [`method Manager.ListDirectObjects`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.ListDirectObjects) - ListDirectObjects returns objects the subject is related to by a stored tuple.
- [`method Manager.ListDirectSubjects`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.ListDirectSubjects) - ListDirectSubjects returns subjects related to an object by a stored tuple.
- [`method Manager.RemoveFromGroup`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.RemoveFromGroup) - RemoveFromGroup removes a user from a group.
- [`method Manager.RequirePermission`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.RequirePermission) - RequirePermission returns an error if the subject does not have the relation.
- [`method Manager.Revoke`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.Revoke) - Revoke removes a relationship tuple.
- [`method Manager.RevokeUserset`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.RevokeUserset) - RevokeUserset removes a userset relationship tuple.
- [`method Manager.SetParent`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/rebac#Manager.SetParent) - SetParent establishes a parent-child relationship between resources.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk`

Package risk provides adaptive risk scoring for authentication and security flows.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk) | [Behavioral guide](./core.md)

- [`type Assessment`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Assessment) - Assessment is the final result of evaluating risk rules.

- [`method Engine.Register`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Engine.Register) - Register adds a rule to the engine.
- [`method Engine.RegisterFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Engine.RegisterFunc) - RegisterFunc adds a function-backed rule to the engine.
- [`type Input`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Input) - Input contains transport-neutral and schema-neutral data for a risk decision.
- [`type Level`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Level) - Level categorizes the severity of an assessment.
- [`const LevelCritical`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#LevelCritical)
- [`const LevelHigh`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#LevelHigh)

- [`func NewFailedAttemptsRule`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#NewFailedAttemptsRule) - NewFailedAttemptsRule increases risk when failed attempts meet or exceed the threshold.
- [`func NewGeoChangedRule`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#NewGeoChangedRule) - NewGeoChangedRule increases risk when geography changed unexpectedly.
- [`func NewImpossibleTripRule`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#NewImpossibleTripRule) - NewImpossibleTripRule increases risk when travel velocity is implausible.
- [`func NewNewDeviceRule`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#NewNewDeviceRule) - NewNewDeviceRule increases risk when the attempt comes from a new device.
- [`type Option`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Option) - Option configures an Engine.
- [`type Rule`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Rule) - Rule evaluates one piece of risk logic.
- [`type RuleFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#RuleFunc) - RuleFunc adapts a function into a Rule.
- [`method RuleFunc.Evaluate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#RuleFunc.Evaluate)
- [`method RuleFunc.Name`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#RuleFunc.Name)
- [`type Signal`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Signal) - Signal captures the outcome of a single rule evaluation.
- [`type Thresholds`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#Thresholds) - Thresholds controls how numeric scores map to risk levels.
- [`func WithThresholds`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#WithThresholds) - WithThresholds overrides the level thresholds.

- [`method thresholdRule.Evaluate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/core/risk#thresholdRule.Evaluate)

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-gorm`

Package gormstore provides a GORM-based storage adapter for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-gorm) | [Behavioral guide](./adapters.md)

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap`

Package ldapstore connects Kayan's LDAP strategy to a real directory server.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap) | [Behavioral guide](./adapters.md)

- [`type Conn`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#Conn) - Conn is an active LDAP connection.
- [`method Conn.Bind`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#Conn.Bind) - Bind authenticates on the connection.
- [`method Conn.BindExternal`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#Conn.BindExternal) - BindExternal authenticates the connection with SASL EXTERNAL, taking the identity from the TLS client certificate (RFC 4422 appendix A).
- [`method Conn.Close`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#Conn.Close) - Close releases the connection and stops the context watcher.
- [`method Conn.Search`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#Conn.Search) - Search executes an LDAP search.
- [`const DefaultTimeout`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#DefaultTimeout) - DefaultTimeout bounds connection and search operations when no timeout is configured.
- [`type Dialer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#Dialer) - Dialer opens TLS connections to an LDAP server.
- [`method Dialer.DialTLS`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#Dialer.DialTLS) - DialTLS implements [flow.LDAPDialer].
- [`type DialerOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#DialerOption) - DialerOption configures a [Dialer].
- [`var ErrNoClientCertificate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#ErrNoClientCertificate) - ErrNoClientCertificate reports a SASL EXTERNAL bind attempted over a connection that presented no client certificate.
- [`var ErrTLSRequired`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#ErrTLSRequired) - ErrTLSRequired reports an attempt to connect without TLS.
- [`func NewDialer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#NewDialer) - NewDialer returns a Dialer that requires TLS 1.2 or better and verifies the server certificate against the platform roots.
- [`func WithInsecureSkipVerify`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#WithInsecureSkipVerify) - WithInsecureSkipVerify disables certificate verification.
- [`func WithRootCAs`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#WithRootCAs) - WithRootCAs trusts pool in addition to the platform roots.
- [`func WithStartTLS`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#WithStartTLS) - WithStartTLS connects in the clear and upgrades the connection with StartTLS before anything else is sent.
- [`func WithTLSConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#WithTLSConfig) - WithTLSConfig replaces the TLS configuration.
- [`func WithTimeout`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-ldap#WithTimeout) - WithTimeout bounds connection and search operations.

- [`const AttrUserAgent`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#AttrUserAgent) - Common span attribute keys.
- [`type Config`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Config) - Config holds the telemetry configuration.
- [`func DefaultConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#DefaultConfig) - DefaultConfig returns a default telemetry configuration.
- [`func EndSpan`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#EndSpan) - EndSpan ends a span with optional error handling.
- [`func NewProvider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#NewProvider) - NewProvider creates a new telemetry provider.
- [`type Provider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider) - Provider manages OpenTelemetry tracer and meter providers.
- [`method Provider.Meter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.Meter) - Meter returns the meter instance.
- [`method Provider.RecordAuthDuration`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.RecordAuthDuration) - RecordAuthDuration records authentication duration.
- [`method Provider.RecordLockout`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.RecordLockout) - RecordLockout records a lockout event.
- [`method Provider.RecordLogin`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.RecordLogin) - RecordLogin records a login attempt.
- [`method Provider.RecordMFA`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.RecordMFA) - RecordMFA records an MFA attempt.
- [`method Provider.RecordRateLimit`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.RecordRateLimit) - RecordRateLimit records a rate limit event.
- [`method Provider.RecordRegistration`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.RecordRegistration) - RecordRegistration records a registration attempt.
- [`method Provider.SessionCreated`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SessionCreated) - SessionCreated increments the active session count.
- [`method Provider.SessionDestroyed`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SessionDestroyed) - SessionDestroyed decrements the active session count.
- [`method Provider.Shutdown`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.Shutdown) - Shutdown gracefully shuts down the telemetry providers.
- [`method Provider.SpanLogin`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanLogin) - SpanLogin starts a span for login operations.
- [`method Provider.SpanMFA`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanMFA) - SpanMFA starts a span for MFA operations.
- [`method Provider.SpanOIDC`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanOIDC) - SpanOIDC starts a span for OIDC operations.
- [`method Provider.SpanPolicyCheck`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanPolicyCheck) - SpanPolicyCheck starts a span for policy evaluation.
- [`method Provider.SpanRateLimit`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanRateLimit) - SpanRateLimit starts a span for rate limit checking.
- [`method Provider.SpanRegistration`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanRegistration) - SpanRegistration starts a span for registration operations.
- [`method Provider.SpanSAML`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanSAML) - SpanSAML starts a span for SAML operations.
- [`method Provider.SpanSessionCreate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanSessionCreate) - SpanSessionCreate starts a span for session creation.
- [`method Provider.SpanSessionRefresh`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanSessionRefresh) - SpanSessionRefresh starts a span for session refresh.
- [`method Provider.SpanSessionValidate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanSessionValidate) - SpanSessionValidate starts a span for session validation.
- [`method Provider.SpanWebAuthn`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.SpanWebAuthn) - SpanWebAuthn starts a span for WebAuthn operations.
- [`method Provider.StartSpan`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.StartSpan) - StartSpan starts a new span with common Kayan attributes.
- [`method Provider.Tracer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Provider.Tracer) - Tracer returns the tracer instance.
- [`func SetSpanError`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#SetSpanError) - SetSpanError marks a span as having an error.
- [`func SetSpanSuccess`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#SetSpanSuccess) - SetSpanSuccess marks a span as successful.
- [`type SpanOptions`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#SpanOptions) - SpanOptions provides configuration for span creation.
- [`func Subscribe`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-observability/telemetry#Subscribe) - Subscribe wires a Provider's metrics to the domain events core/flow already emits, so a deployment gets login, MFA, session, and throttling metrics without any Kayan package importing this one.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2`

Package oauth2 provides OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server functionality for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2) | [Behavioral guide](./oidc-provider.md)

- [`type AuditErrorHandler`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuditErrorHandler) - AuditErrorHandler receives audit persistence failures.
- [`type AuthCode`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthCode) - AuthCode represents a temporary authorization code.
- [`type AuthCodeStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthCodeStore) - AuthCodeStore defines the interface for managing authorization codes.
- [`const AuthMethodClientSecretBasic`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthMethodClientSecretBasic) - AuthMethodClientSecretBasic sends the secret in the Authorization header.
- [`const AuthMethodClientSecretPost`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthMethodClientSecretPost) - AuthMethodClientSecretPost sends the secret in the request body.
- [`const AuthMethodNone`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthMethodNone) - AuthMethodNone is for public clients, which cannot keep a secret.
- [`const AuthMethodPrivateKeyJWT`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthMethodPrivateKeyJWT) - AuthMethodPrivateKeyJWT authenticates the client with a JWT it signed with its own private key (RFC 7523 section 2.2, OpenID Connect Core section 9).
- [`type AuthenticationInfo`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthenticationInfo) - AuthenticationInfo is what the authorization endpoint knew about the sign-in and the token endpoint has to pass on.
- [`type AuthenticationRequirements`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthenticationRequirements) - AuthenticationRequirements are the authentication constraints an authorization request placed on the sign-in (OIDC Core, section 3.1.2.1).
- [`method AuthenticationRequirements.NeedsReauthentication`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthenticationRequirements.NeedsReauthentication) - NeedsReauthentication reports whether an existing session is too old to satisfy max_age.
- [`method AuthenticationRequirements.Requested`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthenticationRequirements.Requested) - Requested reports whether the request placed any constraint.
- [`method AuthenticationRequirements.SatisfiedBy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthenticationRequirements.SatisfiedBy) - SatisfiedBy reports whether an authentication at lastAuth satisfies max_age.
- [`type AuthorizeRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#AuthorizeRequest) - AuthorizeRequest is a validated authorization request.
- [`type Client`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Client) - Client represents an OAuth2 client application.
- [`method Client.AllowsGrantType`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Client.AllowsGrantType) - AllowsGrantType reports whether this client may use the given grant.
- [`method Client.AllowsPostLogoutRedirectURI`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Client.AllowsPostLogoutRedirectURI) - AllowsPostLogoutRedirectURI reports whether uri may be redirected to after this client ends a session.
- [`method Client.AllowsRedirectURI`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Client.AllowsRedirectURI) - AllowsRedirectURI reports whether uri is registered for this client.
- [`method Client.IsPublic`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Client.IsPublic) - IsPublic reports whether the client authenticates with no secret.
- [`type ClientAssertionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ClientAssertionStore) - ClientAssertionStore records the assertion identifiers that have been spent.
- [`const ClientAssertionTypeJWTBearer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ClientAssertionTypeJWTBearer) - ClientAssertionTypeJWTBearer is the only client_assertion_type accepted (RFC 7523 section 2.2).
- [`type ClientKeyResolver`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ClientKeyResolver) - ClientKeyResolver supplies a client's public keys.
- [`type ClientStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ClientStore) - ClientStore defines the interface for managing OAuth2 clients.
- [`const DefaultPushedRequestTTL`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#DefaultPushedRequestTTL) - DefaultPushedRequestTTL is how long a pushed request may be redeemed for.
- [`var ErrAccessDenied`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrAccessDenied) - ErrAccessDenied: the resource owner or authorization server refused the request.
- [`var ErrAssertionReplayed`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrAssertionReplayed) - ErrAssertionReplayed reports a jti that was already used.
- [`var ErrInvalidClient`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrInvalidClient) - ErrInvalidClient: client authentication failed.
- [`var ErrInvalidGrant`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrInvalidGrant) - ErrInvalidGrant: the grant or refresh token is invalid, expired, revoked, or was issued to another client.
- [`var ErrInvalidRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrInvalidRequest) - ErrInvalidRequest: the request is malformed or has a duplicate parameter.
- [`var ErrInvalidScope`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrInvalidScope) - ErrInvalidScope: the requested scope is unknown, malformed, or exceeds what was granted.
- [`var ErrInvalidToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrInvalidToken) - ErrInvalidToken: the access token is expired, revoked, or malformed (RFC 6750 section 3.1).
- [`var ErrNoAssertionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrNoAssertionStore) - ErrNoAssertionStore reports that private_key_jwt is unusable because no replay store was configured.
- [`var ErrNoPushedRequestStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrNoPushedRequestStore) - ErrNoPushedRequestStore reports that PAR is unusable because no store was configured.
- [`var ErrPushedRequestNotFound`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrPushedRequestNotFound) - ErrPushedRequestNotFound reports a request_uri that is unknown, expired, or already redeemed.
- [`var ErrServerError`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrServerError) - ErrServerError: an unexpected condition prevented fulfilling the request.
- [`var ErrTemporarilyUnavailable`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrTemporarilyUnavailable) - ErrTemporarilyUnavailable: the server is overloaded or under maintenance.
- [`var ErrUnauthorizedClient`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrUnauthorizedClient) - ErrUnauthorizedClient: this client may not use this grant type.
- [`var ErrUnsupportedGrantType`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrUnsupportedGrantType) - ErrUnsupportedGrantType: the authorization server does not support this grant.
- [`var ErrUnsupportedResponseType`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ErrUnsupportedResponseType) - ErrUnsupportedResponseType: the authorization server does not support this response type.
- [`type Error`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error) - Error is an OAuth 2.0 protocol error (RFC 6749 sections 4.1.2.1 and 5.2).
- [`method Error.Error`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.Error) - Error implements error.
- [`method Error.Is`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.Is) - Is reports whether target is an *Error with the same code, so that errors.Is(err, oauth2.ErrInvalidGrant) matches a copy carrying a description.
- [`method Error.StatusCode`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.StatusCode) - StatusCode returns the HTTP status this error maps to.
- [`method Error.Unwrap`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.Unwrap) - Unwrap returns the underlying cause, if one was attached.
- [`method Error.WithCause`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.WithCause) - WithCause returns a copy of e wrapping err.
- [`method Error.WithDescription`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.WithDescription) - WithDescription returns a copy of e carrying a client-visible description.
- [`method Error.WithDescriptionf`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.WithDescriptionf) - WithDescriptionf is [Error.WithDescription] with format interpolation.
- [`method Error.WithState`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.WithState) - WithState returns a copy of e echoing the request's state parameter.
- [`method Error.WithURI`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Error.WithURI) - WithURI returns a copy of e pointing at documentation.
- [`const GrantAuthorizationCode`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#GrantAuthorizationCode) - Grant types this provider understands.
- [`const GrantClientCredentials`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#GrantClientCredentials) - Grant types this provider understands.
- [`const GrantRefreshToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#GrantRefreshToken) - Grant types this provider understands.
- [`type IntrospectionResponse`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#IntrospectionResponse) - IntrospectionResponse represents the response according to RFC 7662.
- [`type JWK`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#JWK) - JWK represents a JSON Web Key.
- [`type JWKS`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#JWKS) - JWKS represents a JSON Web Key Set.
- [`type MemoryClientAssertionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#MemoryClientAssertionStore) - MemoryClientAssertionStore is a single-process [ClientAssertionStore].

- [`type MemoryPushedRequestStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#MemoryPushedRequestStore) - MemoryPushedRequestStore is a single-process [PushedRequestStore].

- [`type MemoryRevocationStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#MemoryRevocationStore) - MemoryRevocationStore is an in-memory RevocationStore suitable for testing and single-instance deployments.

- [`func NewMemoryClientAssertionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#NewMemoryClientAssertionStore) - NewMemoryClientAssertionStore creates an empty store.
- [`func NewMemoryPushedRequestStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#NewMemoryPushedRequestStore) - NewMemoryPushedRequestStore creates an empty store.
- [`func NewMemoryRevocationStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#NewMemoryRevocationStore) - NewMemoryRevocationStore creates a new in-memory revocation store.

- [`type Provider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider)
- [`method Provider.ClientCredentials`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.ClientCredentials) - ClientCredentials issues an access token for the client itself.
- [`method Provider.Exchange`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.Exchange) - Exchange exchanges an authorization code for an access token response.
- [`method Provider.GenerateAccessToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.GenerateAccessToken) - GenerateAccessToken generates a signed JWT access token for a user.
