- [`method Provider.GenerateAuthCodeForAuthentication`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.GenerateAuthCodeForAuthentication) - GenerateAuthCodeForAuthentication issues an authorization code and records what the sign-in actually was.
- [`method Provider.Introspect`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.Introspect) - Introspect validates a token and returns its metadata.
- [`method Provider.JWKS`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.JWKS) - JWKS returns the key set to publish at the JWKS endpoint.
- [`method Provider.ParseAuthorizeRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.ParseAuthorizeRequest) - ParseAuthorizeRequest validates authorization request parameters.
- [`method Provider.ParseTokenRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.ParseTokenRequest) - ParseTokenRequest validates a token request and authenticates the client.
- [`method Provider.PushAuthorizationRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.PushAuthorizationRequest) - PushAuthorizationRequest handles the pushed authorization request endpoint (RFC 9126 section 2).
- [`method Provider.Refresh`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.Refresh) - Refresh exchanges a refresh token for a new access token, rotating the refresh token.
- [`method Provider.RequiresPushedRequests`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.RequiresPushedRequests) - RequiresPushedRequests reports whether plain authorization requests are refused.
- [`method Provider.Revoke`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.Revoke) - Revoke invalidates a token by storing its JTI in the revocation store.
- [`method Provider.SupportsPushedRequests`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.SupportsPushedRequests) - SupportsPushedRequests reports whether the PAR endpoint is usable.
- [`method Provider.TokenEndpointAuthMethods`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.TokenEndpointAuthMethods) - TokenEndpointAuthMethods reports the client authentication methods this provider can actually serve.
- [`method Provider.ValidateClient`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#Provider.ValidateClient) - ValidateClient validates the client ID and secret.
- [`type ProviderOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ProviderOption) - ProviderOption configures optional Provider behavior.
- [`func PublicKeyToJWK`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#PublicKeyToJWK) - PublicKeyToJWK converts an RSA public key to a JWK.
- [`type PushedRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#PushedRequest) - PushedRequest is an authorization request a client lodged in advance.
- [`method PushedRequest.ExpiresIn`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#PushedRequest.ExpiresIn) - ExpiresIn returns the seconds remaining, for the endpoint's JSON response.
- [`method PushedRequest.Response`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#PushedRequest.Response) - Response returns the endpoint's JSON body for this pushed request.
- [`method PushedRequest.String`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#PushedRequest.String) - String renders the request for logs without its parameters, which carry state and PKCE values.
- [`type PushedRequestStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#PushedRequestStore) - PushedRequestStore persists pushed authorization requests.
- [`type RefreshToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#RefreshToken) - RefreshToken represents a long-lived token used to obtain new access tokens.
- [`method RefreshToken.IsUsed`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#RefreshToken.IsUsed) - IsUsed reports whether the token has already been redeemed.
- [`type RefreshTokenFamilyStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#RefreshTokenFamilyStore) - RefreshTokenFamilyStore is an optional extension of [RefreshTokenStore] that supports refresh token reuse detection (OAuth 2.1, RFC 9700 section 4.14.2).
- [`type RefreshTokenStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#RefreshTokenStore) - RefreshTokenStore defines the interface for managing refresh tokens.
- [`const RequestURIPrefix`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#RequestURIPrefix) - RequestURIPrefix is the URN scheme a pushed authorization request is addressed by (RFC 9126 section 2.2).
- [`const ResponseTypeCode`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#ResponseTypeCode) - ResponseTypeCode is the only response_type this provider supports: the authorization code flow.
- [`type RevocationStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#RevocationStore) - RevocationStore persists revoked token identifiers for stateless JWT invalidation.
- [`type TokenRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#TokenRequest) - TokenRequest is a validated token request with its client authenticated.
- [`type TokenResponse`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#TokenResponse) - TokenResponse represents the response according to RFC 6749.
- [`func WithAllowPlainCodeChallenge`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithAllowPlainCodeChallenge) - WithAllowPlainCodeChallenge permits the "plain" PKCE method.
- [`func WithClientAssertions`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithClientAssertions) - WithClientAssertions enables private_key_jwt.
- [`func WithClientKeyResolver`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithClientKeyResolver) - WithClientKeyResolver supplies client public keys from somewhere other than [Client.JWKS] -- typically a fetched and cached jwks_uri.
- [`func WithClientSecretHasher`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithClientSecretHasher) - WithClientSecretHasher sets the hasher used to verify client secrets.
- [`func WithKeyProvider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithKeyProvider) - WithKeyProvider supplies the signing keys used for tokens and published in JWKS.
- [`func WithMaxClientAssertionLifetime`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithMaxClientAssertionLifetime) - WithMaxClientAssertionLifetime bounds how far ahead a client assertion may expire.
- [`func WithProviderAudit`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithProviderAudit) - WithProviderAudit enables protocol audit events.
- [`func WithProviderClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithProviderClock) - WithProviderClock sets the clock used for expiry.
- [`func WithPushedRequestTTL`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithPushedRequestTTL) - WithPushedRequestTTL sets how long a request_uri may be redeemed for.
- [`func WithPushedRequests`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithPushedRequests) - WithPushedRequests enables the pushed authorization request endpoint (RFC 9126).
- [`func WithRequirePKCE`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithRequirePKCE) - WithRequirePKCE controls whether an authorization code must carry a PKCE challenge.
- [`func WithRequirePushedRequests`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithRequirePushedRequests) - WithRequirePushedRequests refuses any authorization request that did not arrive through the PAR endpoint.
- [`func WithRevocationStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithRevocationStore) - WithRevocationStore enables token revocation via the supplied store.
- [`func WithTokenEndpointURL`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithTokenEndpointURL) - WithTokenEndpointURL declares this provider's token endpoint.
- [`func WithTokenGenerator`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oauth2#WithTokenGenerator) - WithTokenGenerator sets the source of authorization codes and refresh tokens.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc`

Package oidc provides OpenID Connect (OIDC) server functionality for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc) | [Behavioral guide](./oidc-provider.md)

- [`type BackChannelLogoutNotifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#BackChannelLogoutNotifier) - BackChannelLogoutNotifier implements the OIDC Back-Channel Logout notification.
- [`method BackChannelLogoutNotifier.NotifyClient`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#BackChannelLogoutNotifier.NotifyClient)
- [`method BackChannelLogoutNotifier.NotifyLogout`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#BackChannelLogoutNotifier.NotifyLogout)
- [`type BackChannelLogoutOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#BackChannelLogoutOption) - BackChannelLogoutOption configures optional notifier behavior.
- [`type ClaimsSource`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ClaimsSource) - ClaimsSource supplies the claims for an identity.
- [`type ClaimsSourceFunc`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ClaimsSourceFunc) - ClaimsSourceFunc adapts a function to [ClaimsSource].
- [`method ClaimsSourceFunc.Claims`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ClaimsSourceFunc.Claims) - Claims implements [ClaimsSource].
- [`type ClientAuthMethodSource`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ClientAuthMethodSource) - ClientAuthMethodSource reports which client authentication methods a token endpoint can serve.
- [`type ClientLister`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ClientLister) - ClientLister is a consumer-defined interface for listing OAuth2 clients.
- [`const DefaultIDTokenTTL`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#DefaultIDTokenTTL) - DefaultIDTokenTTL is how long an ID token is valid when no TTL is given.
- [`type Discovery`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Discovery) - Discovery represents the OIDC discovery metadata.
- [`type DiscoveryOptions`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#DiscoveryOptions) - DiscoveryOptions describes what a deployment supports.
- [`type EndSessionRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#EndSessionRequest) - EndSessionRequest is a validated RP-initiated logout request (OpenID Connect RP-Initiated Logout 1.0, section 2).
- [`method EndSessionRequest.RedirectURL`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#EndSessionRequest.RedirectURL) - RedirectURL returns the URL to send the user to after the session ends.
- [`type Endpoints`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Endpoints) - Endpoints locates the routes a deployment serves.
- [`var ErrClientListingUnavailable`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ErrClientListingUnavailable)
- [`var ErrInsufficientScope`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ErrInsufficientScope) - ErrInsufficientScope is returned when the token is valid but was not granted the openid scope.
- [`var ErrInvalidIDTokenHint`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ErrInvalidIDTokenHint) - ErrInvalidIDTokenHint reports an id_token_hint that is absent where required, malformed, or not signed by this provider.
- [`var ErrInvalidToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ErrInvalidToken) - ErrInvalidToken is returned when the presented access token is missing, inactive, or does not identify a person.
- [`var ErrMaxAgeNotSatisfied`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ErrMaxAgeNotSatisfied) - ErrMaxAgeNotSatisfied reports an ID token that would have ignored a max_age the authorization request carried.
- [`var ErrNoClientStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ErrNoClientStore) - ErrNoClientStore reports that logout cannot be served because no client store is configured.
- [`var ErrPostLogoutRedirectNotRegistered`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ErrPostLogoutRedirectNotRegistered) - ErrPostLogoutRedirectNotRegistered reports a post_logout_redirect_uri that is not on the identified client's allowlist.
- [`var ErrUnknownClient`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ErrUnknownClient) - ErrUnknownClient reports that the request named a client this provider does not know.
- [`type IDTokenRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#IDTokenRequest) - IDTokenRequest describes an ID token to issue.
- [`type LogoutNotifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#LogoutNotifier) - LogoutNotifier defines the interface for notifying clients of a logout event.
- [`func NewBackChannelLogoutNotifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#NewBackChannelLogoutNotifier)
- [`func NewServer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#NewServer)
- [`type PushedRequestSource`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#PushedRequestSource) - PushedRequestSource reports whether a provider serves pushed authorization requests.
- [`type Server`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Server)
- [`method Server.BuildDiscovery`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Server.BuildDiscovery) - BuildDiscovery assembles the OpenID Provider metadata document.
- [`method Server.GenerateIDToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Server.GenerateIDToken) - GenerateIDToken generates a signed OIDC ID Token.
- [`method Server.GetDiscovery`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Server.GetDiscovery) - GetDiscovery returns a discovery document with conventional endpoint paths.
- [`method Server.IssueIDToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Server.IssueIDToken) - IssueIDToken mints an ID token for a request.
- [`method Server.ParseEndSessionRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Server.ParseEndSessionRequest) - ParseEndSessionRequest validates an RP-initiated logout request.
- [`method Server.UserInfo`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#Server.UserInfo) - UserInfo returns the claims for the identity an access token authenticates.
- [`type ServerOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#ServerOption) - ServerOption configures a [Server].
- [`type TokenIntrospector`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#TokenIntrospector) - TokenIntrospector reports what an access token authorizes.
- [`func WithClaimsSource`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithClaimsSource) - WithClaimsSource supplies the claims placed in ID tokens.
- [`func WithClientAuthMethods`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithClientAuthMethods) - WithClientAuthMethods lets discovery advertise the client authentication methods the token endpoint actually serves.
- [`func WithClientStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithClientStore) - WithClientStore supplies the client store RP-initiated logout validates against.
- [`func WithPushedRequestSupport`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithPushedRequestSupport) - WithPushedRequestSupport lets discovery advertise the RFC 9126 endpoint according to what the provider actually serves.
- [`func WithServerAllowPlainCodeChallenge`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithServerAllowPlainCodeChallenge) - WithServerAllowPlainCodeChallenge records that the provider accepts the "plain" PKCE method, so discovery advertises it.
- [`func WithServerClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithServerClock) - WithServerClock sets the clock used for token timestamps.
- [`func WithServerKeyProvider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithServerKeyProvider) - WithServerKeyProvider supplies the signing keys, so discovery can advertise the algorithms actually in use and JWKS can publish them.
- [`func WithStrictClientListing`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithStrictClientListing) - WithStrictClientListing makes NotifyLogout fail when the client store does not implement ClientLister instead of silently skipping fan-out.
- [`func WithTokenIntrospector`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-oidc-provider/oidc#WithTokenIntrospector) - WithTokenIntrospector supplies the token validator [Server.UserInfo] uses.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis`

Package kredis provides Redis-backed storage adapters for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis) | [Behavioral guide](./adapters.md)

- [`func NewRedisLockoutStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#NewRedisLockoutStore) - NewRedisLockoutStore creates a new Redis-based lockout store.
- [`func NewRedisRateLimiter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#NewRedisRateLimiter) - NewRedisRateLimiter creates a new Redis-based rate limiter.
- [`func NewRedisSSOStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#NewRedisSSOStore) - NewRedisSSOStore creates an optional Redis-backed session.SSOStore.
- [`func NewRedisSessionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#NewRedisSessionStore) - NewRedisSessionStore creates a new Redis-backed session store.
- [`func NewRedisWebAuthnSessionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#NewRedisWebAuthnSessionStore) - NewRedisWebAuthnSessionStore creates a new Redis-based WebAuthn session store.
- [`type RedisLockoutStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisLockoutStore) - RedisLockoutStore implements flow.LockoutStore using Redis for distributed deployments.
- [`method RedisLockoutStore.ClearFailures`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisLockoutStore.ClearFailures) - ClearFailures resets the failure count for the identifier.
- [`method RedisLockoutStore.IsLocked`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisLockoutStore.IsLocked) - IsLocked checks if the identifier is currently locked.
- [`method RedisLockoutStore.Lock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisLockoutStore.Lock) - Lock manually locks the identifier for the given duration.
- [`method RedisLockoutStore.RecordFailure`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisLockoutStore.RecordFailure) - RecordFailure increments the failure count for the identifier.
- [`type RedisRateLimiter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisRateLimiter) - RedisRateLimiter implements flow.RateLimiter using Redis for distributed rate limiting.
- [`method RedisRateLimiter.Allow`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisRateLimiter.Allow) - Allow checks if the request should be allowed using sliding window log algorithm.
- [`method RedisRateLimiter.Reset`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisRateLimiter.Reset) - Reset clears the rate limit counter for the given key.
- [`type RedisSSOStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSSOStore) - RedisSSOStore persists cross-application sessions in Redis.
- [`method RedisSSOStore.CreateOrJoinSSOSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSSOStore.CreateOrJoinSSOSession)
- [`method RedisSSOStore.DeactivateSSOSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSSOStore.DeactivateSSOSession)
- [`method RedisSSOStore.DeleteSSOSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSSOStore.DeleteSSOSession)
- [`method RedisSSOStore.GetSSOSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSSOStore.GetSSOSession)
- [`method RedisSSOStore.GetSSOSessionByIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSSOStore.GetSSOSessionByIdentity)
- [`method RedisSSOStore.JoinSSOSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSSOStore.JoinSSOSession)
- [`method RedisSSOStore.LeaveSSOSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSSOStore.LeaveSSOSession)
- [`type RedisSessionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSessionStore) - RedisSessionStore implements domain.SessionStorage using Redis.
- [`method RedisSessionStore.CreateSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSessionStore.CreateSession) - CreateSession stores a session in Redis as a hash with a refresh token mapping.
- [`method RedisSessionStore.DeleteSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSessionStore.DeleteSession) - DeleteSession removes a session and its refresh token mapping.
- [`method RedisSessionStore.GetSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSessionStore.GetSession) - GetSession retrieves a session by its ID.
- [`method RedisSessionStore.GetSessionByRefreshToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisSessionStore.GetSessionByRefreshToken) - GetSessionByRefreshToken looks up a session by its refresh token.
- [`type RedisWebAuthnSessionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisWebAuthnSessionStore) - RedisWebAuthnSessionStore implements flow.WebAuthnSessionStore using Redis.
- [`method RedisWebAuthnSessionStore.DeleteSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisWebAuthnSessionStore.DeleteSession)
- [`method RedisWebAuthnSessionStore.GetSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisWebAuthnSessionStore.GetSession)
- [`method RedisWebAuthnSessionStore.SaveSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#RedisWebAuthnSessionStore.SaveSession)
- [`type SessionStoreOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#SessionStoreOption) - SessionStoreOption configures a RedisSessionStore.
- [`func WithSessionPrefix`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#WithSessionPrefix) - WithSessionPrefix sets the key prefix for session data.
- [`func WithSessionTTL`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-redis#WithSessionTTL) - WithSessionTTL sets the TTL for session keys.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml`

Package saml provides SAML 2.0 Service Provider functionality for Kayan IAM.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml) | [Behavioral guide](./saml.md)

- [`type Assertion`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Assertion) - Assertion represents a SAML assertion (simplified).
- [`type Attribute`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Attribute) - Attribute represents a SAML attribute.
- [`type AttributeStatement`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AttributeStatement) - AttributeStatement contains user attributes.
- [`type AttributeValue`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AttributeValue) - AttributeValue holds an attribute value.
- [`type AudienceRestriction`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AudienceRestriction) - AudienceRestriction limits which service providers may accept an assertion.
- [`type AuthnContext`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AuthnContext) - AuthnContext describes the authentication method used.
- [`const AuthnContextMFA`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AuthnContextMFA) - AuthnContextMFA is the multi-factor class.
- [`const AuthnContextPassword`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AuthnContextPassword) - AuthnContextPassword is a password over an unprotected transport.
- [`const AuthnContextPasswordProtectedTransport`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AuthnContextPasswordProtectedTransport) - AuthnContextPasswordProtectedTransport is a password over TLS.
- [`const AuthnContextX509`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AuthnContextX509) - AuthnContextX509 is a certificate-based authentication.
- [`type AuthnRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AuthnRequest) - AuthnRequest represents a SAML authentication request.
- [`type AuthnStatement`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#AuthnStatement) - AuthnStatement records how and when the subject authenticated.
- [`const BindingHTTPPost`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#BindingHTTPPost) - BindingHTTPPost is the HTTP-POST binding, used for responses and assertions because they exceed what a URL can carry.
- [`const BindingHTTPRedirect`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#BindingHTTPRedirect) - BindingHTTPRedirect is the HTTP-Redirect binding, used for requests.
- [`const ComparisonBetter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ComparisonBetter) - Comparison values for RequestedAuthnContext (SAML 2.0 Core, section 3.3.2.2.1).
- [`const ComparisonExact`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ComparisonExact) - Comparison values for RequestedAuthnContext (SAML 2.0 Core, section 3.3.2.2.1).
- [`const ComparisonMaximum`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ComparisonMaximum) - Comparison values for RequestedAuthnContext (SAML 2.0 Core, section 3.3.2.2.1).
- [`const ComparisonMinimum`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ComparisonMinimum) - Comparison values for RequestedAuthnContext (SAML 2.0 Core, section 3.3.2.2.1).
- [`type Conditions`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Conditions) - Conditions for assertion validity.
- [`type Config`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Config) - Config holds SAML Service Provider configuration.
- [`const ConfirmationMethodBearer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ConfirmationMethodBearer) - ConfirmationMethodBearer is the bearer confirmation method used by web SSO.
- [`type Decrypter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Decrypter) - Decrypter opens an EncryptedAssertion.
- [`const DefaultClockSkew`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#DefaultClockSkew) - DefaultClockSkew is the tolerance applied to assertion validity windows.
- [`type EncryptedAssertion`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#EncryptedAssertion) - EncryptedAssertion is an assertion the identity provider encrypted to this service provider's public key.
- [`type EncryptedID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#EncryptedID) - EncryptedID is a name identifier the identity provider encrypted to this service provider's public key (SAML 2.0 Core, section 2.2.4).
- [`type EntityDescriptor`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#EntityDescriptor)
- [`var ErrAmbiguousNameID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrAmbiguousNameID) - ErrAmbiguousNameID reports a subject carrying both a plaintext NameID and an EncryptedID.
- [`var ErrAssertionExpired`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrAssertionExpired) - ErrAssertionExpired reports an assertion outside its validity window.
- [`var ErrAssertionNotYetValid`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrAssertionNotYetValid) - ErrAssertionNotYetValid reports an assertion whose NotBefore is ahead.
- [`var ErrAuthnContextNotSatisfied`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrAuthnContextNotSatisfied) - ErrAuthnContextNotSatisfied reports an assertion whose authentication context is not one the request asked for.
- [`var ErrDecryption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrDecryption) - ErrDecryption is returned when an encrypted assertion cannot be opened.
- [`var ErrEncryptedIDUnreadable`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrEncryptedIDUnreadable) - ErrEncryptedIDUnreadable reports an EncryptedID whose plaintext is not a NameID.
- [`var ErrInvalidSignature`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrInvalidSignature) - ErrInvalidSignature reports a signature that did not verify.
- [`var ErrMissingAssertionID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrMissingAssertionID) - ErrMissingAssertionID reports an assertion with no ID, which cannot be tracked for replay.
- [`var ErrNoDecrypter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrNoDecrypter) - ErrNoDecrypter is returned when a response carries an EncryptedAssertion and no decrypter is configured.
- [`var ErrNoMetadataSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrNoMetadataSigner) - ErrNoMetadataSigner reports that a signed metadata document was requested but no signer is configured.
- [`var ErrNoRedirectSignature`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrNoRedirectSignature) - ErrNoRedirectSignature reports a redirect-binding message carrying no detached signature.
- [`var ErrNoRedirectSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrNoRedirectSigner) - ErrNoRedirectSigner is returned when signing is required but no signer is available.
- [`var ErrNoSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrNoSigner) - ErrNoSigner reports that signing was requested with no signer configured.
- [`var ErrNoSuchIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrNoSuchIdentity) - ErrNoSuchIdentity reports a valid assertion for a NameID that has no identity here, when automatic provisioning is not enabled.
- [`var ErrNoVerifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrNoVerifier) - ErrNoVerifier reports that verification was requested with no verifier.
- [`var ErrReplay`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrReplay) - ErrReplay reports an assertion ID that has been seen before.
- [`var ErrServiceProviderNotSigned`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrServiceProviderNotSigned) - ErrServiceProviderNotSigned reports a registered service provider with no certificate.
- [`var ErrStaleAuthentication`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrStaleAuthentication) - ErrStaleAuthentication reports an assertion whose AuthnInstant predates the request that asked for a fresh authentication.
- [`var ErrUnknownServiceProvider`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrUnknownServiceProvider) - ErrUnknownServiceProvider reports a logout message from an entity this identity provider does not know.
- [`var ErrUnsigned`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrUnsigned) - ErrUnsigned reports a document carrying no signature.
- [`var ErrUnsolicited`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrUnsolicited) - ErrUnsolicited reports an unsolicited response where one was not allowed.
- [`var ErrUnsupportedAlgorithm`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrUnsupportedAlgorithm) - ErrUnsupportedAlgorithm is returned for an encryption algorithm this implementation does not accept.
- [`var ErrWrongAudience`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrWrongAudience) - ErrWrongAudience reports an assertion minted for a different service provider.
- [`var ErrWrongDestination`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrWrongDestination) - ErrWrongDestination reports an assertion delivered to the wrong endpoint.
- [`var ErrWrongIssuer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ErrWrongIssuer) - ErrWrongIssuer reports an assertion from an unexpected identity provider.
- [`type HTTPDoer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#HTTPDoer) - HTTPDoer is the minimal HTTP client contract used for metadata retrieval.
- [`type Hooks`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Hooks) - Hooks provides extension points for SAML flow customization.
- [`type IDPSSODescriptor`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IDPSSODescriptor)
- [`type IdPConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdPConfig) - IdPConfig represents an external Identity Provider configuration.
- [`type IdPHooks`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdPHooks) - IdPHooks provides extension points for IdP operations.
- [`type IdPLogoutInstruction`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdPLogoutInstruction) - IdPLogoutInstruction is a verified logout request from a service provider.
- [`type IdPOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdPOption) - IdPOption configures an [IdentityProvider].
- [`type IdPServerConfig`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdPServerConfig) - IdPServerConfig holds configuration for Kayan acting as a SAML IdP.

- [`method IdentityProvider.BuildLogoutResponse`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdentityProvider.BuildLogoutResponse) - BuildLogoutResponse produces the signed answer owed to a service provider once its logout request has been carried out.

- [`method IdentityProvider.HandleSSORequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdentityProvider.HandleSSORequest) - HandleSSORequest processes an incoming SSO request from an SP.
- [`method IdentityProvider.LogoutTargets`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdentityProvider.LogoutTargets) - LogoutTargets lists the registered service providers that can be sent a logout request, excluding the one named.
- [`method IdentityProvider.PostBindingForm`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdentityProvider.PostBindingForm) - PostBindingForm renders the HTML form that delivers a SAML response to the service provider over the HTTP-POST binding.
- [`method IdentityProvider.ProcessLogoutRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdentityProvider.ProcessLogoutRequest) - ProcessLogoutRequest verifies a service provider's LogoutRequest and reports whose sessions to end.
- [`method IdentityProvider.RegisterSP`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdentityProvider.RegisterSP) - RegisterSP adds a Service Provider.
- [`method IdentityProvider.SetHooks`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdentityProvider.SetHooks) - SetHooks sets lifecycle hooks.
- [`method IdentityProvider.SignedMetadata`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#IdentityProvider.SignedMetadata) - SignedMetadata returns this identity provider's metadata with an enveloped XML-DSig signature over the EntityDescriptor.
- [`type Issuer`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Issuer) - Issuer represents the SAML issuer element.
- [`type KeyDescriptor`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#KeyDescriptor)
- [`type KeyInfo`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#KeyInfo)
- [`type LoginOptions`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#LoginOptions) - LoginOptions are the per-login authentication options of an AuthnRequest.
- [`type LogoutInstruction`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#LogoutInstruction) - LogoutInstruction is a verified request to end a local session.
- [`type LogoutRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#LogoutRequest) - LogoutRequest asks a party to end a subject's session (SAML 2.0 Core section 3.7.1).
- [`type LogoutResponse`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#LogoutResponse) - LogoutResponse answers a [LogoutRequest] (SAML 2.0 Core section 3.7.3).
- [`type LogoutSubject`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#LogoutSubject) - LogoutSubject names whose session to end.

- [`func NewMemoryReplayCache`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#NewMemoryReplayCache) - NewMemoryReplayCache returns an empty in-process replay cache.

- [`func NewRSARedirectSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#NewRSARedirectSigner) - NewRSARedirectSigner returns a signer for the given key and algorithm.

- [`func NewXMLDSigVerifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#NewXMLDSigVerifier) - NewXMLDSigVerifier returns a verifier that requires a valid signature.

- [`func ParseRedirectBinding`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ParseRedirectBinding) - ParseRedirectBinding decodes a SAML message from HTTP-Redirect query parameters.

- [`type RSARedirectSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#RSARedirectSigner) - RSARedirectSigner signs redirect-binding queries with an RSA key.
- [`method RSARedirectSigner.SignRedirect`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#RSARedirectSigner.SignRedirect) - SignRedirect implements [RedirectSigner].
- [`type RedirectSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#RedirectSigner) - RedirectSigner signs a message for the HTTP-Redirect binding.
- [`type ReplayCache`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ReplayCache) - ReplayCache records assertion IDs that have been accepted.
- [`type RequestedAuthnContext`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#RequestedAuthnContext) - RequestedAuthnContext asks the identity provider for a particular kind of authentication (SAML 2.0 Core, section 3.3.2.2.1).
- [`type Response`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Response) - Response represents a SAML response (simplified).
- [`type SAMLUser`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SAMLUser) - SAMLUser represents user information extracted from a SAML assertion.
- [`method SAMLUser.GetAttribute`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SAMLUser.GetAttribute) - GetAttribute returns the first value of an attribute.
- [`method SAMLUser.GetAttributes`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SAMLUser.GetAttributes) - GetAttributes returns all values of an attribute.
- [`type SPOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SPOption) - SPOption configures a [ServiceProvider].
- [`type SPRegistration`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SPRegistration) - SPRegistration represents a registered Service Provider.

- [`method ServiceProvider.BuildLogoutResponse`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.BuildLogoutResponse) - BuildLogoutResponse produces the reply owed to an identity provider once the local session has been ended.
- [`method ServiceProvider.GetIdP`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.GetIdP) - GetIdP returns the IdP configuration.
- [`method ServiceProvider.GetMetadata`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.GetMetadata) - GetMetadata returns this service provider's SAML metadata document.
- [`method ServiceProvider.InitiateLogin`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.InitiateLogin) - InitiateLogin starts the SAML authentication flow.
- [`method ServiceProvider.InitiateLoginWith`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.InitiateLoginWith) - InitiateLoginWith starts the SAML authentication flow with per-login authentication options, and returns the redirect URL to the IdP.
- [`method ServiceProvider.InitiateLogout`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.InitiateLogout) - InitiateLogout builds a redirect asking an identity provider to end a subject's session everywhere it is signed in.
- [`method ServiceProvider.ProcessLogoutRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.ProcessLogoutRequest) - ProcessLogoutRequest verifies an identity provider's LogoutRequest and reports whose session to end.
- [`method ServiceProvider.ProcessRedirectLogoutRequest`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.ProcessRedirectLogoutRequest) - ProcessRedirectLogoutRequest verifies a LogoutRequest that arrived over the HTTP-Redirect binding and reports whose session to end.

- [`method ServiceProvider.SignedMetadata`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ServiceProvider.SignedMetadata) - SignedMetadata returns this service provider's metadata with an enveloped XML-DSig signature over the EntityDescriptor.

- [`type SessionStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SessionStore) - SessionStore interface for SAML session persistence.
- [`const SigAlgRSASHA1`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SigAlgRSASHA1) - SigAlgRSASHA1 is refused.
- [`const SigAlgRSASHA256`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SigAlgRSASHA256) - SigAlgRSASHA256 is the default and what identity providers expect.
- [`const SigAlgRSASHA512`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#SigAlgRSASHA512) - SigAlgRSASHA512 is accepted for deployments that require it.

- [`type Status`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Status) - Status represents the SAML status.
- [`type StatusCode`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#StatusCode) - StatusCode represents the status code.
- [`const StatusPartialLogout`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#StatusPartialLogout) - StatusPartialLogout reports that some sessions could not be ended (SAML 2.0 Core section 3.7.3.2).
- [`const StatusResponder`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#StatusResponder) - StatusResponder reports that the responder could not carry out the request (SAML 2.0 Core section 3.2.2.2).
- [`const StatusSuccess`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#StatusSuccess) - StatusSuccess is the SAML status code for a successful response.

- [`method Strategy.Authenticate`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Strategy.Authenticate) - Authenticate is not directly used for SAML (it's redirect-based).

- [`method Strategy.ID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Strategy.ID)
- [`type Subject`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#Subject) - Subject contains the NameID.

- [`type ValidationOptions`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#ValidationOptions) - ValidationOptions carries what an assertion is checked against.

- [`func WithAutoProvision`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithAutoProvision) - WithAutoProvision allows a successful assertion from a NameID with no existing identity to create one.
- [`func WithDecrypter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithDecrypter) - WithDecrypter enables encrypted assertions.
- [`func WithIDAttribute`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithIDAttribute) - WithIDAttribute sets the attribute used to resolve signature references.
- [`func WithIdPClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithIdPClock) - WithIdPClock sets the clock used for assertion validity windows.
- [`func WithIdPDecrypter`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithIdPDecrypter) - WithIdPDecrypter enables encrypted name identifiers in incoming logout requests, for federations that encrypt subject identifiers.
- [`func WithIdPSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithIdPSigner) - WithIdPSigner sets the signer used for outgoing assertions.
- [`func WithIdPVerifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithIdPVerifier) - WithIdPVerifier sets the verifier for incoming service provider messages.

- [`func WithMetadataURLPolicy`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithMetadataURLPolicy) - WithMetadataURLPolicy replaces the default public-HTTPS metadata policy.
- [`func WithRedirectSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithRedirectSigner) - WithRedirectSigner supplies the signer for HTTP-Redirect binding messages.
- [`func WithReplayCache`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithReplayCache) - WithReplayCache replaces the assertion replay cache.
- [`func WithSPClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithSPClock)
- [`func WithSPSigner`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithSPSigner) - WithSPSigner sets the signer for enveloped XML-DSig signatures, used on outgoing LogoutResponse documents.
- [`func WithSignatureVerifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#WithSignatureVerifier) - WithSignatureVerifier replaces the signature verifier.
- [`type X509Data`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#X509Data)

- [`method XMLDSigSigner.Sign`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#XMLDSigSigner.Sign) - Sign implements [Signer].
- [`type XMLDSigVerifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-saml#XMLDSigVerifier) - XMLDSigVerifier verifies signatures with goxmldsig.

## `github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing`

Package kayantesting provides in-memory implementations of Kayan's storage contracts, plus a contract test suite any implementation can run.

[Package documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing) | [Behavioral guide](./adapters.md)

- [`func AuditStoreSuite`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#AuditStoreSuite) - AuditStoreSuite runs persistence, filtering, pagination, export, and purge behavior required by [audit.AuditStore].
- [`func CredentialStorageSuite`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#CredentialStorageSuite) - CredentialStorageSuite runs the credential create, lookup, and update contract.
- [`type CredentialStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#CredentialStore) - CredentialStore is the complete credential contract exercised by the suite.
- [`var ErrAlreadyExists`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#ErrAlreadyExists) - Errors reported by [MemoryStore].
- [`var ErrNotFound`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#ErrNotFound) - Errors reported by [MemoryStore].
- [`type FakeClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#FakeClock) - FakeClock is a [domain.Clock] whose time only moves when told.
- [`method FakeClock.Advance`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#FakeClock.Advance) - Advance moves the clock forward by d.
- [`method FakeClock.Now`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#FakeClock.Now) - Now returns the current fake time.
- [`method FakeClock.Set`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#FakeClock.Set) - Set moves the clock to t.
- [`func IdentityStorageSuite`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#IdentityStorageSuite) - IdentityStorageSuite runs the identity contract with [SuiteIdentity].
- [`func IdentityStorageSuiteWithModel`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#IdentityStorageSuiteWithModel) - IdentityStorageSuiteWithModel runs the identity contract with a caller model.
- [`type MemoryStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore) - MemoryStore is an in-memory [domain.Storage].
- [`method MemoryStore.ConsumeToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.ConsumeToken) - ConsumeToken atomically retrieves and removes a live token of the expected type.
- [`method MemoryStore.Count`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.Count) - Count returns the number of audit events matching filter.
- [`method MemoryStore.CreateCredential`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.CreateCredential) - CreateCredential stores cred, which must be an *identity.Credential.
- [`method MemoryStore.CreateIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.CreateIdentity) - CreateIdentity stores ident, which must expose a non-empty ID.
- [`method MemoryStore.CreateSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.CreateSession) - CreateSession stores a session and indexes it by refresh token.
- [`method MemoryStore.DeleteExpiredTokens`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.DeleteExpiredTokens) - DeleteExpiredTokens removes every token whose expiry has passed.
- [`method MemoryStore.DeleteIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.DeleteIdentity) - DeleteIdentity removes the identity with the given ID.
- [`method MemoryStore.DeleteSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.DeleteSession) - DeleteSession removes a session and its refresh-token index entry.
- [`method MemoryStore.DeleteToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.DeleteToken) - DeleteToken removes a token.
- [`method MemoryStore.Events`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.Events) - Events returns a copy of every recorded audit event, for assertions.
- [`method MemoryStore.Export`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.Export) - Export returns matching events as JSON or CSV.
- [`method MemoryStore.FindIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.FindIdentity) - FindIdentity returns the first identity whose fields all match query.
- [`method MemoryStore.GetCredentialByIdentifier`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.GetCredentialByIdentifier) - GetCredentialByIdentifier returns the credential for an identifier and method.
- [`method MemoryStore.GetIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.GetIdentity) - GetIdentity returns the identity with the given ID.
- [`method MemoryStore.GetSession`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.GetSession) - GetSession returns the session with the given ID.
- [`method MemoryStore.GetSessionByRefreshToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.GetSessionByRefreshToken) - GetSessionByRefreshToken returns the session holding the given refresh token.
- [`method MemoryStore.GetToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.GetToken) - GetToken returns a token by value.
- [`method MemoryStore.ListIdentities`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.ListIdentities) - ListIdentities returns one page of identities.
- [`method MemoryStore.Purge`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.Purge) - Purge deletes audit events recorded before olderThan and returns the count.
- [`method MemoryStore.Query`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.Query) - Query returns audit events matching filter.
- [`method MemoryStore.Reset`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.Reset) - Reset empties the store, so one instance can serve several subtests.
- [`method MemoryStore.SaveEvent`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.SaveEvent) - SaveEvent appends an audit event.
- [`method MemoryStore.SaveToken`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.SaveToken) - SaveToken stores a transient authentication token.
- [`method MemoryStore.UpdateCredentialSecret`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.UpdateCredentialSecret) - UpdateCredentialSecret replaces the secret on every credential belonging to an identity for the given method.
- [`method MemoryStore.UpdateIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStore.UpdateIdentity) - UpdateIdentity replaces the stored identity with the same ID.
- [`type MemoryStoreOption`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#MemoryStoreOption) - MemoryStoreOption configures a [MemoryStore].
- [`func NewFakeClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#NewFakeClock) - NewFakeClock returns a clock fixed at t.
- [`func NewMemoryStore`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#NewMemoryStore) - NewMemoryStore returns an empty in-memory store.
- [`func SessionStorageSuite`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#SessionStorageSuite) - SessionStorageSuite runs the session lifecycle contract.
- [`func StorageSuite`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#StorageSuite) - StorageSuite runs the [domain.Storage] contract against an implementation.
- [`func StorageSuiteWithModel`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#StorageSuiteWithModel) - StorageSuiteWithModel is [StorageSuite] for a store whose identity model is not [SuiteIdentity].
- [`type SuiteIdentity`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#SuiteIdentity) - SuiteIdentity is the identity model the contract suite stores.
- [`method SuiteIdentity.GetID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#SuiteIdentity.GetID) - GetID satisfies the identity contract used by the flow packages.
- [`method SuiteIdentity.SetID`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#SuiteIdentity.SetID) - SetID satisfies the identity contract used by the flow packages.
- [`func TokenStoreSuite`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#TokenStoreSuite) - TokenStoreSuite runs the transient-token lifecycle contract.
- [`func WithClock`](https://pkg.go.dev/github.com/getkayan/kayan/kayan-testing#WithClock) - WithClock sets the clock used for timestamps.
